## 1. Introduction

Multimodal remote sensing images are typically obtained from observations of the same object <sub>a</sub>t dif<sub>eren</sub>t ti<sub>mes</sub> <sub>us</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> dif<sub>eren</sub>t <sub>sensors</sub> <sub>an</sub>d i<sub>mag</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> <sub>mec</sub>h<sub>an</sub>i<sub>sms,</sub> <sub>suc</sub>h <sub>as</sub> <sub>op</sub>ti<sub>ca</sub>l<sub>-</sub>i<sub>n</sub>f<sub>rare</sub>d i<sub>mage</sub> (Li et al. 2022b), o<sub>p</sub>tical-h<sub>yp</sub>ers<sub>p</sub>ectral ima<sub>g</sub>e (Wan<sub>g</sub> et al. 2024b) , o<sub>p</sub>tical-SAR ima<sub>g</sub>e (Kulkarni and Re<sub>g</sub>e 2020), o<sub>p</sub>tical-LiDAR ima<sub>g</sub>e (Wan<sub>g</sub> et al. 2023), o<sub>p</sub>tical-ni<sub>g</sub>httime li<sub>g</sub>ht ima<sub>g</sub>e and o<sub>p</sub>tical-ma<sub>p</sub> ima<sub>g</sub>e (Jian<sub>g</sub> et al. 2021). As shown in Fi<sub>g</sub>ure 1.

![](images/4c310707b2e289a4e618d90b7111702d25f03fcec31d40b021eea7f570af5f99.jpg)  
Figure 1. Multimodal remote sensing image pairs.

Compared with single-modal sensors, the joint processing of multimodal remote sensing data <sub>y</sub>ields more reliable, com<sub>p</sub>rehensive, and accurate observation results (Brown 1992). Consequentl<sub>y</sub>, <sub>researc</sub>h <sub>on</sub> <sub>mu</sub>lti<sub>mo</sub>d<sub>a</sub>l i<sub>mage</sub> <sub>reg</sub>i<sub>s</sub>t<sub>ra</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> i<sub>s</sub> <sub>o</sub>f <sub>s</sub>i<sub>gn</sub>ifi<sub>can</sub>t <sub>sc</sub>i<sub>en</sub>tifi<sub>c</sub> i<sub>mpor</sub>t<sub>ance.</sub> F<sub>ur</sub>th<sub>ermore,</sub> <sub>reg</sub>i<sub>s</sub>t<sub>ra</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> <sub>serves</sub> <sub>as</sub> <sub>a</sub> f<sub>un</sub>d<sub>amen</sub>t<sub>a</sub>l <sub>prerequ</sub>i<sub>s</sub>it<sub>e</sub> f<sub>or</sub> <sub>numerous</sub> d<sub>owns</sub>t<sub>ream</sub> <sub>app</sub>li<sub>ca</sub>ti<sub>ons,</sub> <sub>suc</sub>h as ima<sub>g</sub>e fusion (Tan<sub>g</sub> et al. 2023b), chan<sub>g</sub>e detection (Sun et al. 2024), and object detection (Belmouhcine et al. 2023). Ultimatel , efective multi-level and multifaceted Earth observation relies on the thorou<sub>g</sub>h inte<sub>g</sub>ration of these diverse data sources (Tan<sub>g</sub> et al. 2023a).

B<sub>ase</sub>d <sub>on</sub> di<sub>s</sub>ti<sub>nc</sub>t <sub>reg</sub>i<sub>s</sub>t<sub>ra</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> <sub>processes,</sub> t<sub>ra</sub>diti<sub>ona</sub>l <sub>me</sub>th<sub>o</sub>d<sub>s</sub> <sub>are</sub> <sub>pr</sub>i<sub>mar</sub>il<sub>y</sub> <sub>c</sub>l<sub>ass</sub>ifi<sub>e</sub>d i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>o</sub> <sub>reg</sub>i<sub>on-</sub> based and feature-based a<sub>pp</sub>roaches (Sommervold et al. 2023). (2011) <sub>p</sub>ro<sub>p</sub>osed Uniform Robust SIFT (UR-SIFT), utilizin<sub>g</sub> stabilit<sub>y</sub> and <sub>s</sub>i<sub>gn</sub>ifi<sub>cance cons</sub>t<sub>ra</sub>i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>s</sub> t<sub>o ex</sub>t<sub>rac</sub>t <sub>un</sub>if<sub>orm</sub>l<sub>y</sub> di<sub>s</sub>t<sub>r</sub>ib<sub>u</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d f<sub>ea</sub>t<sub>ures across</sub> b<sub>o</sub>th <sub>sca</sub>l<sub>e an</sub>d i<sub>mage spaces.</sub> Buildin<sub>g</sub> u<sub>p</sub>on this, the<sub>y</sub> later introduced Ada<sub>p</sub>tive Binnin<sub>g</sub> SIFT (AB-SIFT) (Seda<sub>g</sub>hat and Ebadi 2015), which em<sub>p</sub>lo<sub>y</sub>s an ada<sub>p</sub>tive histo<sub>g</sub>ram quantization strate<sub>gy</sub> to <sub>g</sub>enerate descri<sub>p</sub>tors more <sub>ro</sub>b<sub>us</sub>t <sub>aga</sub>i<sub>ns</sub>t l<sub>oca</sub>l <sub>geome</sub>t<sub>r</sub>i<sub>c</sub> di<sub>s</sub>t<sub>or</sub>ti<sub>ons.</sub>

## 3.3. Feature registration method based on phase consistency

Ye et al. (2018b) <sub>p</sub>ioneered the use of the illumination- and contrast-invariant PC model for feature detection and descri<sub>p</sub>tion. Their MMPC-La<sub>p</sub> (Minimum Moment of Phase Con<sub>g</sub>ruenc<sub>y</sub> La<sub>p</sub>) detector utilizes auto-scale localization to identif<sub>y</sub> stable ke<sub>yp</sub>oints in scale s<sub>p</sub>ace, while the LHOPC (Local Histo<sub>g</sub>ram of Oriented Phase Con<sub>g</sub>ruenc<sub>y</sub>) descri<sub>p</sub>tor ado<sub>p</sub>ts a DAISY-like s<sub>p</sub>atial confi<sub>g</sub>uration to maintain robustness under com<sub>p</sub>lex radiometric variations. Li et al. (2020) introduced the Radiation-variation Insensitive Feature Transform (RIFT), which detects feature <sub>p</sub>oints on PC minimum/maximum moment ma<sub>p</sub>s and constructs a Maximum Index Ma<sub>p</sub> (MIM) <sub>v</sub>i<sub>a</sub> l<sub>og-</sub>G<sub>a</sub>b<sub>or</sub> <sub>convo</sub>l<sub>u</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> <sub>sequences,</sub> <sub>s</sub>i<sub>gn</sub>ifi<sub>can</sub>tl<sub>y</sub> i<sub>mprov</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> <sub>ra</sub>di<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> i<sub>nvar</sub>i<sub>ance.</sub> T<sub>o</sub> f<sub>ur</sub>th<sub>er</sub> enhance matchin<sub>g</sub>, Yao et al. (2021) <sub>p</sub>ro<sub>p</sub>osed an anisotro<sub>p</sub>ic wei<sub>g</sub>hted moment ma<sub>p</sub> for detection, cou<sub>p</sub>led with the Histo<sub>g</sub>ram of Absolute Phase Con<sub>g</sub>ruenc<sub>y</sub> Gradients (HAPCG) within a lo<sub>g</sub>-<sub>p</sub>olar <sub>s</sub>t<sub>ruc</sub>t<sub>ure</sub> f<sub>or</sub> d<sub>escr</sub>i<sub>p</sub>ti<sub>on.</sub>

(2019b) <sub>p</sub>ro<sub>p</sub>osed a coarse-to-fine f<sub>ramewor</sub>k<sub>.</sub> It <sub>u</sub>tili<sub>zes</sub> <sub>a</sub> VGG<sub>-</sub>16 b<sub>ac</sub>kb<sub>one</sub> f<sub>or</sub> <sub>pyram</sub>id<sub>a</sub>l f<sub>ea</sub>t<sub>ure</sub> d<sub>e</sub>t<sub>ec</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> t<sub>o</sub> <sub>approx</sub>i<sub>ma</sub>t<sub>e</sub> <sub>spa</sub>ti<sub>a</sub>l <sub>re</sub>l<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>ons</sub>hi<sub>ps,</sub> f<sub>o</sub>ll<sub>owe</sub>d b<sub>y a com</sub>bi<sub>na</sub>ti<sub>on o</sub>f d<sub>eep an</sub>d h<sub>an</sub>d<sub>cra</sub>ft<sub>e</sub>d f<sub>ea</sub>t<sub>ures</sub> f<sub>or re</sub>fi<sub>ne</sub>d t<sub>rans</sub>f<sub>orma</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> <sub>es</sub>ti<sub>ma</sub>ti<sub>on.</sub>

Advancin<sub>g</sub> be<sub>y</sub>ond sim<sub>p</sub>le feature re<sub>p</sub>lacement, Lan et al. Eficienc<sub>y</sub> and <sub>p</sub>recision have also been ke<sub>y</sub> drivers of innovation. Xian<sub>g</sub> et al. Ex<sub>p</sub>lorin<sub>g</sub> further h<sub>y</sub>bridizations, CTNet (Yue et al. 2025) successfull<sub>y</sub> fuses CNN-based local information with Transformer-based <sub>g</sub>l<sub>o</sub>b<sub>a</sub>l <sub>con</sub>t<sub>ex</sub>t<sub>s.</sub> B<sub>eyon</sub>d <sub>pure</sub>l<sub>y s</sub>t<sub>ruc</sub>t<sub>ura</sub>l i<sub>nnova</sub>ti<sub>ons, recen</sub>t <sub>a</sub>d<sub>vancemen</sub>t<sub>s</sub> h<sub>ave a</sub>l<sub>so em</sub>b<sub>e</sub>dd<sub>e</sub>d <sub>p</sub>h<sub>ys</sub>i<sub>ca</sub>l <sub>cons</sub>t<sub>ra</sub>i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>s</sub> i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>o</sub> T<sub>rans</sub>f<sub>ormer</sub> <sub>arc</sub>hit<sub>ec</sub>t<sub>ures.</sub> F<sub>or</sub> i<sub>ns</sub>t<sub>ance,</sub> <sub>com</sub>bi<sub>n</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> <sub>geograp</sub>hi<sub>c</sub> l<sub>oca</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> <sub>a</sub>tt<sub>en</sub>ti<sub>on w</sub>ith <sub>p</sub>h<sub>ase cons</sub>i<sub>s</sub>t<sub>ency</sub> h<sub>as proven</sub> hi<sub>g</sub>hl<sub>y e</sub>f<sub>ec</sub>ti<sub>ve</sub> i<sub>n s</sub>i<sub>gn</sub>ifi<sub>can</sub>tl<sub>y</sub> i<sub>mprov</sub>i<sub>ng genera</sub>li<sub>za-</sub> tion and matchin<sub>g</sub> accurac<sub>y</sub> for com<sub>p</sub>lex multi-source SAR ima<sub>g</sub>er<sub>y</sub> (Gao et al. 2025b). Rather than desi<sub>g</sub>nin<sub>g</sub> <sub>mo</sub>d<sub>a</sub>lit<sub>y-spec</sub>ifi<sub>c</sub> <sub>mo</sub>d<sub>u</sub>l<sub>es,</sub> MINIMA l<sub>everages</sub> <sub>a</sub> <sub>genera</sub>ti<sub>ve</sub> d<sub>a</sub>t<sub>a</sub> <sub>eng</sub>i<sub>ne</sub> t<sub>o</sub> t<sub>rans</sub>f<sub>orm</sub> <sub>eas</sub>il<sub>y</sub> <sub>o</sub>bt<sub>a</sub>i<sub>n-</sub> able RGB data into a com<sub>p</sub>rehensive s<sub>y</sub>nthetic dataset (MD-s<sub>y</sub>n) encom<sub>p</sub>assin<sub>g</sub> diverse modalities. B<sub>y</sub> di<sub>rec</sub>tl<sub>y</sub> t<sub>ra</sub>i<sub>n</sub>i<sub>ng a</sub>d<sub>vance</sub>d <sub>p</sub>i<sub>pe</sub>li<sub>nes on</sub> th<sub>ese ran</sub>d<sub>om</sub>l<sub>y se</sub>l<sub>ec</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d <sub>mo</sub>d<sub>a</sub>lit<sub>y pa</sub>i<sub>rs,</sub> th<sub>e</sub>i<sub>r</sub> f<sub>ramewor</sub>k <sub>ac</sub>hi<sub>eves</sub> <sub>excep</sub>ti<sub>ona</sub>l <sub>zero-s</sub>h<sub>o</sub>t <sub>cross-mo</sub>d<sub>a</sub>l <sub>genera</sub>li<sub>za</sub>ti<sub>on,</sub> <sub>success</sub>f<sub>u</sub>ll<sub>y</sub> b<sub>r</sub>id<sub>g</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> <sub>severe</sub> <sub>seman</sub>ti<sub>c</sub> <sub>gaps</sub> <sub>across</sub> <sub>var</sub>i<sub>ous</sub> h<sub>e</sub>t<sub>erogeneous</sub> <sub>scenar</sub>i<sub>os.</sub> Ulti<sub>ma</sub>t<sub>e</sub>l<sub>y,</sub> th<sub>ese</sub> <sub>s</sub>t<sub>u</sub>di<sub>es</sub> <sub>co</sub>ll<sub>ec</sub>ti<sub>ve</sub>l<sub>y</sub> hi<sub>g</sub>hli<sub>g</sub>ht th<sub>e</sub> <sub>evo</sub>l<sub>v</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> l<sub>an</sub>d<sub>scape</sub> <sub>o</sub>f <sub>mu</sub>lti<sub>mo</sub>d<sub>a</sub>l <sub>reg</sub>i<sub>s</sub>t<sub>ra</sub>ti<sub>on,</sub> <sub>progress</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> f<sub>rom</sub> <sub>comp</sub>l<sub>ex</sub> <sub>a</sub>tt<sub>en</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> <sub>mec</sub>h<sub>an</sub>i<sub>sms</sub> t<sub>o</sub> hi<sub>g</sub>hl<sub>y</sub> <sub>sca</sub>l<sub>a</sub>bl<sub>e,</sub> d<sub>a</sub>t<sub>a-cen</sub>t<sub>r</sub>i<sub>c</sub> l<sub>earn</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> <sub>para</sub>di<sub>gms.</sub>

I<sub>n</sub> <sub>summary,</sub> l<sub>earn</sub>i<sub>ng-</sub>b<sub>ase</sub>d f<sub>ea</sub>t<sub>ure</sub> <sub>ma</sub>t<sub>c</sub>hi<sub>ng</sub> <sub>s</sub>i<sub>gn</sub>ifi<sub>can</sub>tl<sub>y</sub> i<sub>mproves</sub> d<sub>e</sub>t<sub>ec</sub>t<sub>or</sub> <sub>repea</sub>t<sub>a</sub>bilit<sub>y</sub> <sub>an</sub>d d<sub>escr</sub>i<sub>p</sub>t<sub>or ro</sub>b<sub>us</sub>t<sub>ness</sub> b<sub>y</sub> l<sub>everag</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> hi<sub>g</sub>h<sub>-</sub>di<sub>mens</sub>i<sub>ona</sub>l <sub>seman</sub>ti<sub>c represen</sub>t<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>ons.</sub> H<sub>owever,</sub> it i<sub>s</sub> <sub>no</sub>t<sub>ewor</sub>th<sub>y</sub> th<sub>a</sub>t <sub>curren</sub>t <sub>researc</sub>h i<sub>n</sub> <sub>mu</sub>lti<sub>mo</sub>d<sub>a</sub>l <sub>reg</sub>i<sub>s</sub>t<sub>ra</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> <sub>rema</sub>i<sub>ns</sub> <sub>pre</sub>d<sub>om</sub>i<sub>nan</sub>tl<sub>y</sub> f<sub>ocuse</sub>d <sub>on</sub> f<sub>ea-</sub> t<sub>ure</sub> d<sub>escr</sub>i<sub>p</sub>ti<sub>on, w</sub>ith <sub>re</sub>l<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>ve</sub>l<sub>y</sub> l<sub>ess exp</sub>l<sub>ora</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>o spec</sub>i<sub>a</sub>li<sub>ze</sub>d f<sub>ea</sub>t<sub>ure</sub> d<sub>e</sub>t<sub>ec</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> f<sub>or</sub> h<sub>e</sub>t<sub>erogeneous</sub> d<sub>a</sub>t<sub>a.</sub>

## (iii) Modality Unification Methods Based on Style Transfer

T<sub>o m</sub>iti<sub>ga</sub>t<sub>e</sub> th<sub>e</sub> i<sub>mpac</sub>t <sub>o</sub>f <sub>s</sub>i<sub>gn</sub>ifi<sub>can</sub>t <sub>non</sub>li<sub>near ra</sub>di<sub>ome</sub>t<sub>r</sub>i<sub>c</sub> dif<sub>erences, genera</sub>ti<sub>ve mo</sub>d<sub>e</sub>l<sub>s suc</sub>h as Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) have been introduced to the field. These a<sub>pp</sub>roaches <sub>ac</sub>hi<sub>eve mo</sub>d<sub>a</sub>lit<sub>y un</sub>ifi<sub>ca</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> th<sub>roug</sub>h <sub>s</sub>t<sub>y</sub>l<sub>e</sub> t<sub>rans</sub>f<sub>er, su</sub>b<sub>sequen</sub>tl<sub>y emp</sub>l<sub>oy</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> t<sub>ra</sub>diti<sub>ona</sub>l <sub>reg</sub>i<sub>on- or</sub> f<sub>ea</sub>t<sub>ure-</sub>b<sub>ase</sub>d t<sub>ec</sub>h<sub>n</sub>i<sub>ques</sub> t<sub>o</sub> <sub>reg</sub>i<sub>s</sub>t<sub>er</sub> th<sub>e</sub> t<sub>rans</sub>f<sub>orme</sub>d i<sub>mages.</sub> S<sub>pec</sub>ifi<sub>ca</sub>ll<sub>y,</sub> th<sub>ese</sub> <sub>me</sub>th<sub>o</sub>d<sub>s</sub> l<sub>everage</sub> i<sub>mage-</sub>t<sub>o-</sub>i<sub>mage</sub> t<sub>rans</sub>l<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> t<sub>o</sub> <sub>ensure</sub> th<sub>a</sub>t th<sub>e</sub> <sub>mu</sub>lti<sub>mo</sub>d<sub>a</sub>l i<sub>mages</sub> <sub>ex</sub>hibit <sub>cons</sub>i<sub>s</sub>t<sub>en</sub>t <sub>ra</sub>di<sub>ome</sub>t<sub>r</sub>i<sub>c</sub> characteristics and structural features. For instance, Merkle et al. (2018) utilized Conditional GANs (CGANs) to <sub>g</sub>enerate <sub>p</sub>seudo-SAR ima<sub>g</sub>e <sub>p</sub>atches from o<sub>p</sub>tical data, achievin<sub>g</sub> su<sub>p</sub>erior matchin<sub>g</sub> accurac<sub>y</sub> usin<sub>g</sub> Normalized Cross-Correlation (NCC) and SIFT features. (2019b) extended visual attribute transfer b<sub>y</sub> fusin<sub>g</sub> ori<sub>g</sub>inal structure and texture info, th<sub>ere</sub>b<sub>y</sub> <sub>wea</sub>k<sub>en</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> <sub>non</sub>li<sub>near</sub> <sub>ra</sub>di<sub>ome</sub>t<sub>r</sub>i<sub>c</sub> di<sub>screpanc</sub>i<sub>es</sub> <sub>an</sub>d <sub>e</sub>f<sub>ec</sub>ti<sub>ve</sub>l<sub>y</sub> i<sub>mprov</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> th<sub>e</sub> <sub>prec</sub>i<sub>s</sub>i<sub>on</sub> <sub>o</sub>f t<sub>ra</sub>diti<sub>ona</sub>l f<sub>ea</sub>t<sub>ure-</sub>b<sub>ase</sub>d <sub>ma</sub>t<sub>c</sub>hi<sub>ng.</sub>

Zhao et al. (2022) <sub>p</sub>ro<sub>p</sub>osed a Smooth C<sub>y</sub>cle-Consistent GAN with a refined loss function to i<sub>mprove</sub> th<sub>e qua</sub>lit<sub>y o</sub>f<sub>syn</sub>th<sub>es</sub>i<sub>ze</sub>d i<sub>mages.</sub> B<sub>y emp</sub>l<sub>oy</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> D2<sub>-</sub>N<sub>e</sub>t <sub>an</sub>d L<sub>o</sub>FTR f<sub>or ma</sub>t<sub>c</sub>hi<sub>ng</sub> b<sub>e</sub>t<sub>ween</sub> <sub>s</sub>t<sub>y</sub>l<sub>e-</sub>t<sub>rans</sub>f<sub>erre</sub>d <sub>an</sub>d <sub>or</sub>i<sub>g</sub>i<sub>na</sub>l <sub>aer</sub>i<sub>a</sub>l i<sub>mages,</sub> th<sub>ey e</sub>f<sub>ec</sub>ti<sub>ve</sub>l<sub>y en</sub>h<sub>ance</sub>d <sub>reg</sub>i<sub>s</sub>t<sub>ra</sub>ti<sub>on accuracy an</sub>d robustness.
